Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on parsing structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 939. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 157 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the parsing decomposition. By minimality, the element a^939 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 157-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)
